{# Integration notes: - Save in templates/auth/. Both files extend the existing templates/base.html. - Original auth endpoints, POST field names and csrf_token() are preserved. - All CSS and JavaScript is inline and scoped; no Tailwind or toast dependency. - Server handlers remain responsible for validation, authentication, roles and CSRF. - Optional trusted URL variables: password_reset_url, biometric_login_url, terms_url and privacy_url. Supply your application's existing routes when available. - Without those URLs, help dialogs explain the next step; no reset, biometric capture, sign-in simulation or device access is performed in browser code. - Policies, role authorization and biometric consent/enrollment are handled server-side. - Posted non-secret fields are retained; password values are never rendered back. - Embedded photos: https://www.pexels.com/photo/5301699/ (team) and https://www.pexels.com/photo/7362929/ (merchant). - Provider logos retain their original artwork. Source assets are the same M-PESA, Safaricom, DPO/Network, Finserve, Pesapal and DHL marks as the homepage. - Inline styling/scripts and data: image sources follow the existing template model. #} {% endblock %}
